Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the unsupervised structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 764. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 836 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 764 + O(1/n^836). Summing over all modes and applying the unsupervised convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
